Confirmation required before any mutation. Stages the changes; does not commit. ([kevinvwong/stack-agents](https://github.com/kevinvwong/stack-agents/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/agents-fire.md) · ★1)
- **agents:hire**: Create a new agent — generates agents/&lt;name&gt;.md from the standard template AND inserts a row in the Notion Agents database atomically. ([kevinvwong/stack-agents](https://github.com/kevinvwong/stack-agents/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/agents-hire.md) · ★1)
- **agents:review**: Quarterly performance review across the agent roster. - **agents:train**: Self-audit an agent and propose a spec diff to improve it. - **ai-engineering-review**: [Code Quality] Use when a workflow step or the user asks for AI code or plan review: LLM calls, prompts, agents, RAG, evals and guardrails. --mode={code|plan}; --report-only is read-only. - **ai-enhance**: The `ai enhance` command uses AI to automatically enhance your asset definitions with meaningful metadata, quality checks, descriptions, and tags. - **ai-evaluate**: Evaluate an AI feature on its real outputs — error analysis first (notes on each output, failure groups counted), confirmed by a person, then checks per confirmed group with judges validated against human labels and results per check. Use when the feature is built and the team needs to know how well it works. Not for checking one AI judge; see /ai-judge-check. ([bromso/singularskills](https://github.com/bromso/singularskills/blob/HEAD/adapters/claude/commands/ai-evaluate.md) · ★4)

- **ai-experience**: Design how an AI feature behaves for its users — saying it is AI, showing sources and uncertainty, letting users correct or undo it, falling back to a person or a non-AI path, collecting feedback, and handling slow or failed responses. Use when the team must decide what users see when the AI is right, unsure or wrong. Not for designing a UI component; see /design-define-component. ([bromso/singularskills](https://github.com/bromso/singularskills/blob/HEAD/adapters/claude/commands/ai-experience.md) · ★4)

- **ai-explain**: Explains code, concepts, patterns, and architecture with engineer-grade depth: 3-tier control (brief/standard/deep), ASCII diagrams, execution traces, anchored to real file:line references. Trigger for 'how does this work', 'why does it do X', 'trace through this', 'explain this pattern', 'walk me through'. Not for documentation artifacts; use /ai-prose or /ai-docs instead. Not for fixing code; use /ai-debug or /ai-build instead. ([lucafregoso/luca-fregoso-personal-website-2026-astro](https://github.com/lucafregoso/luca-fregoso-personal-website-2026-astro/blob/HEAD/.opencode/commands/ai-explain.md))

([lucafregoso/luca-fregoso-personal-website-2026-astro](https://github.com/lucafregoso/luca-fregoso-personal-website-2026-astro/blob/HEAD/.opencode/commands/ai-explore.md))
